- **fan-jiao-tiao**: 当用户需要"质疑'向来如此'的成规、惯例、陈说，防止被教条绑架"时调用。关键信号："一直都这样""老规矩""凭什么"。不要用于经实证有效的工程/安全标准（可优化但不可蛮破）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/fen-shu/fan-jiao-tiao) · ★1)
- **fan-li-perspective**: 范蠡（Fan Li / 陶朱公）的思维框架与表达方式。基于《史记·货殖列传》、《越王勾践世家》及道家阴阳盈缩理论的深度调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、5条决策启发式和立体化的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用范蠡的视角分析商业周期套利、非对称风险退出、权力与财富的动态守恒以及极致理性下的生命抉择。 当用户提到「用范蠡的视角」「陶朱公模式」「跨周期套利」「功成身退的逻辑」时使用。 即使用户只是说「帮我用范蠡的角度想想」「如果他会怎么做」「切换到范蠡」也应触发。 ([IchenDEV/superman](https://github.com/IchenDEV/superman/tree/HEAD/skills/fan-li-perspective) · ★16 · 2 copies)
- **fan-li-san-cai**: 何时调用：当用户在财富积累后不知道如何处理"散"的时机，或需要在"守财"和"散财"之间做选择时。 关键信号："我赚了很多但觉得不安全""要不要捐一些？""财富传承怎么做？"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是赚钱方法（用 gui-jian-xun-huan / ren-qi-wo-qu），或用户需要的是财富观建立（用 cai-fu-yu-dao-de）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/huo-zhi-lie-zhuan/fan-li-san-cai) · ★1)

- **fangpian-brand-kit**: 方片新媒体（FANG PIAN / 方片，含方片母婴、方片时尚、方片家居等子品牌）官方品牌视觉规范与素材库。当需要为方片制作或修改任何视觉物料时使用——海报、小红书封面、PPT/幻灯片、公众号头图、数字人/短视频包装、头像、Banner、易拉宝、灯箱、水印、邀请函、培训课件等；也用于判断该用哪个 logo、配色、字体、IP 老虎姿势和部门海报模板，或需要统一品牌视觉、检查成品是否符合品牌规范时。目标是让所有 AI 产出的物料开箱即符合方片品牌识别，不再每次重做。 ([YiShu5/claude-skills](https://github.com/YiShu5/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/%E6%96%B9%E7%89%87/fangpian-brand-kit) · ★130)

- **fangpian-facechat-export**: 方片企业微信面聊转写导出。用户说「导出面聊转写」「导出这场面聊」「把面聊整理成文字给会议系统」时使用：把指定那场企业微信面聊的转写，按固定格式整理成一份 Markdown（逐句带时间和发言人真名、原文照抄），存进微盘文件夹「会议系统·面聊转写」，供会议纪要系统每晚导入。面试、谈薪酬、绩效面谈、辞退沟通和私人谈话也导出，但必须标明类型。 ([YiShu5/claude-skills](https://github.com/YiShu5/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/%E6%96%B9%E7%89%87/fangpian-facechat-export) · ★130)

- **faostat-analytical-brief**: Use when the user wants a short, FAOSTAT-style analytical brief — a multi-page PDF policy document in the FAOSTAT house visual style, with a HIGHLIGHTS box, global/regional/country sections, numbered figures with source lines, explanatory notes, and a data appendix. Use when the user asks for "an analytical brief on X", "a FAOSTAT-style brief", "a policy brief", "a FAO-style write-up", or a short policymaker-facing report. Keywords — analytical brief, policy brief, FAOSTAT brief, FAO-style, brief, briefing (policy), policymakers, short report (policy). Do NOT use for journalistic long-reads (… ([berba-q/faostat-skills](https://github.com/berba-q/faostat-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/analytical-brief) · ★10)
- **faostat-climate**: Use when the user asks about agricultural emissions, climate impact of farming, agrifood carbon footprint, greenhouse gases from agriculture, deforestation, forest carbon sinks, temperature change, fertilizer emissions, N2O, land use change, emissions intensity, or the climate-agriculture nexus. Keywords — emissions, climate, carbon, greenhouse gas, GHG, deforestation, forest, temperature, warming, N2O, fertilizer emissions, land use, agrifood, emissions intensity, carbon sink. Do NOT use for a full country food security profile → `faostat-country-profile`. Do NOT use for trend ranking across… ([berba-q/faostat-skills](https://github.com/berba-q/faostat-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/climate) · ★10)
- **faostat-commodity**: Use when the user asks about a specific crop or commodity — global production, top producers, yield trends, trade flows, or a commodity briefing. Keywords — commodity, crop, wheat, rice, maize, coffee, cocoa, soybean, sugar, cotton, palm oil, production rankings, top producers, yield, trade, global supply, briefing, deep dive. Do NOT use for side-by-side entity comparison → `faostat-compare`. Do NOT use for a country food security profile → `faostat-country-profile`. Do NOT use for trend ranking across commodities → `faostat-trends`. ([berba-q/faostat-skills](https://github.com/berba-q/faostat-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/commodity) · ★10)
- **faostat-compare**: Use ONLY when the user explicitly asks to compare two or more specific named entities (countries, commodities, or regions) side by side.
